[@dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller](../index.md) / Roll

# Class: Roll\<T\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:72](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L72)

One evaluated notation: the result tree and its totals.

Can be used on its own, or through a `Roller`, which adds rolls made by `roll()` to its history.

`total` is calculated straight away; `minTotal` / `maxTotal` evaluate the expression again in
`min` / `max` mode on first access, and are cached (or taken from the data on import). The
readonly properties do not make the result tree or shared settings deeply immutable.

## Example

```ts
const roll = new Roll("4d6kh3");   // rolls straight away
roll.total;                        // e.g. 14
`${roll}`;                         // e.g. "4d6kh3: [3d, 5, 6, 3] = 14"

new Roll("4d6", { engine: engines.MersenneTwister19937.seed(42) });
```

## Type Parameters

### T

`T` = `string`

the output type of the configured presenter

## Constructors

### Constructor

> **new Roll**\<`T`\>(`input`, `options?`): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:94](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L94)

Parses (if a string) and rolls the input. `RollData` is restored as it is, without rolling or
validating: its result and totals are used, and `reroll()` rolls its expression. Use
`Roll.import()` / `Roll.fromJSON()` for exported data, which validate it.

#### Parameters

##### input

`string` \| [`ExpressionNode`](../type-aliases/ExpressionNode.md) \| [`RollData`](../interfaces/RollData.md)

the notation or expression to roll, or data to restore without validation

##### options?

[`RollSettings`](RollSettings.md)\<`T`\> \| [`RollOptions`](../interfaces/RollOptions.md)\<`T`\>

how to roll; `RollSettings` are used as they are, so they can be shared

#### Returns

`Roll`\<`T`\>

#### Throws

the notation is invalid

### total

> `readonly` **total**: `number`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:78](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L78)

The total, rounded to 2 decimal places.

## Accessors

### averageTotal

#### Get Signature

> **get** **averageTotal**(): `number`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:180](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L180)

The midpoint of `minTotal` and `maxTotal`, as in v5.

This is not the statistical mean for notations like `4d6kh3` or exploding dice.

##### Returns

`number`

### maxTotal

#### Get Signature

> **get** **maxTotal**(): `number`

Exports as JSON (default), a JSON-safe object, or UTF-8 base64 JSON. Evaluates uncached min/max totals.

### reroll()

> **reroll**(): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:185](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L185)

Rolls the original input again with the same settings; restored rolls use their stored expression. Does not add to a history.

`Roll`\<`T`\>

### toString()

> **toString**(): `string`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:197](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L197)

***

### fromJSON()

> `static` **fromJSON**\<`T`\>(`data`, `options?`): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:128](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/e5fc57b800027d5376a4b67c69ac191796693323/src/Roll.ts#L128)

Creates a roll from exported data (e.g. from `toJSON()`), without rolling.

The options are used by `reroll()`. Pools refer to their die by factory name, so re-rolling
uses the die registered under that name in these settings.

#### Type Parameters

##### T

`T` = `string`
